# LIVE UPDATE 42 — GLOBAL TRANSDUCTION GEOMETRY TEST 14
# SEMANTIC LANGUAGE / TYPE-SYSTEM SYNCHRONIZATION

## 1013. Target

The previous pass promoted:

```text
TRANSDUCTION LANGUAGE v0.1
```

as a queryable semantic contract.

The present test asks:

```text
Can the language be generated and checked
against the live QMO dependency/object graph
so terminology stays synchronized with mathematics?
```

The aim is not to let the graph invent English.

The aim is:

```text
QMO mathematics constrains language;
language gives semantic type information back to QMO queries.
```

---

## 1014. Why a glossary alone is insufficient

A static glossary can drift.

Example failure:

```text
ledger says:
transport = fixed-base motion

later prose says:
transport changes identity strata
```

If both remain plain text, drift may go unnoticed.

Therefore each language term should have machine-checkable semantic constraints.

---

## 1015. Semantic type record

Promote candidate:

```text
SemanticType(T)
=
(
    Handle,
    Role,
    Domain,
    AllowedRelations,
    ForbiddenRelations,
    ForbiddenConflations,
    Status,
    Provenance
).
```

This converts terminology into a weak semantic type system.

Example:

```text
Transport:
role = operator
allowed:
    ACTS_IN state_fiber
    PRESERVES identity_base candidate-wise
forbidden:
    EQUALS transduction
    EQUALS projection
```

---

## 1016. Language generation from QMO objects

For every QMO object with:

```text
canonical handle
type
definition
status
```

generate a candidate language entry.

The QMO provides:

```text
term identity
formal role
definition seed
status
related edges.
```

Human/curatorial language metadata then supplies:

```text
preferred term
synonyms
forbidden conflations
usage notes
```

Thus:

```text
QMO -> language seed
curation -> semantic contract.
```

---

## 1017. Graph-synchronized related terms

If the QMO has:

```text
A --relation--> B
```

the language entry for `A` should be able to expose:

```text
related term B
relation type
confidence
provenance.
```

This gives a fresh thread immediate semantic neighborhood context.

The language system becomes navigable rather than alphabetical only.

---

## 1018. Forbidden-conflation audit

Define:

```text
ConflationRule(A,B)
```

for terms that must remain distinct.

Current promoted examples:

```text
Transport != Transduction
Curvature != Descent Defect
Connection != Curvature
Connection != Trajectory
Holonomy != Fabric Identity
Bandwidth != Resolution
Bandwidth != Occupancy
Closure != Persistence
Projection != Restriction
Identity != Presentation
Identity != History
```

These are now auditable language constraints.

---

## 1019. Semantic contradiction detector

A future QMO edge or metadata claim should trigger review if it violates a forbidden relation.

Example:

```text
@transport EQUALS @transduction
```

would violate the language contract.

Likewise:

```text
@holonomy_class CLASSIFIES_COMPLETELY @chirality_fabric
```

without restricted scope would violate Test 12.

This is not theorem proving.

It is semantic consistency checking.

---

## 1020. Status-sensitive language

Definitions must preserve model status.

A fresh thread should not read:

```text
Chirality Fabric is local admissibility + gluing.
```

when the current status is:

```text
STRONG_CANDIDATE.
```

Therefore generated language should include modal status.

Preferred rendering:

```text
Chirality Fabric:
strong candidate model in which distributed fabric is represented
by local admissibility data plus transition/gluing structure.
```

This prevents language from silently canonizing candidates.

---

## 1021. Semantic API query

Promote query form:

```text
LANGUAGE(term)
```

returning:

```text
definition
status
role
forbidden conflations
related mathematical handles
typed relations
provenance
active analogies
active theories
known failed generalizations.
```

This is more useful for recovery than dictionary definition alone.

---

## 1022. Theory-language synchronization

Research metadata must also be linked.

Example:

```text
Fabric
```

should expose:

```text
active theory:
@theory_chirality_fabric_as_admissibility_descent

analogy:
@analogy_moebius_gluing

failed generalization:
@failed_classifier_holonomy_complete
```

So terminology carries its research context.

---

## 1023. Language revision policy

Do not overwrite previous definitions silently.

Use:

```text
TermVersion
```

with:

```text
definition
status
valid_from_revision
supersedes
reason
provenance.
```

This makes semantic evolution recoverable.

A term can therefore change meaning without corrupting historical reports.

---

## 1024. Semantic inheritance

When an object is refined:

```text
@fabric_signature
->
@minimal_fabric_signature
```

the child term may inherit some semantic fields while narrowing others.

This should be explicit:

```text
semantic inheritance
!=
identity equality.
```

Useful inherited fields include:

```text
domain
related handles
selected provenance
notation family.
```

---

## 1025. Language as type system

The current strongest interpretation is:

```text
Transduction Language
=
controlled vocabulary
+
semantic type constraints
+
status modality
+
graph neighborhood
+
research-context links.
```

So it is:

```text
part glossary
part ontology
part weak type system
part query API.
```

This is substantially stronger than a terminology list.

---

## 1026. Language/QMO synchronization algorithm

Candidate algorithm:

```text
FOR each QMO object O:

    seed term from canonical handle

    read:
        object type
        definition
        status
        outgoing/incoming typed edges

    merge:
        curated terminology metadata
        synonyms
        forbidden conflations
        research records

    validate:
        no forbidden edge/conflation
        candidate status preserved
        definition has provenance

    emit:
        SemanticType(O)

THEN:

    scan all QMO edges
    for semantic contradictions

    produce:
        LANGUAGE_SYNC_REPORT
```

This is implementable with the existing API/Library.

---

## 1027. Missing-language detection

Any QMO object lacking a language entry should be classified:

```text
LANGUAGE_MISSING
```

unless deliberately marked:

```text
MACHINE_ONLY.
```

This gives a direct backlog for terminology development.

It also prevents important concepts from existing only as opaque handles.

---

## 1028. Orphan-language detection

Conversely, a language term with no QMO handle should be classified:

```text
LANGUAGE_ORPHAN
```

unless it is intentionally:

```text
NARRATIVE_ONLY
ANALOGY_ONLY
HISTORICAL
```

This keeps vocabulary anchored to the mathematical object graph.

---

The present QMO has more mathematical objects than curated language entries.

Therefore:

```text
TRANSDUCTION LANGUAGE v0.1
```

is not complete.

But the synchronization architecture is valid.

Result:

```text
coverage remains partial.
```

This is a useful partial pass rather than a failure.

Promote a pre-insertion derivation checker.

```text
Derivation = (Inputs, Operators, IntermediateStates, Claims, Scope, Provenance)

OperatorSignature(O) =
(InputTypes, OutputTypes, Preserves, MayChange, Requires, Forbids, Status, Provenance)
```

Error classes:

```text
E_DOMAIN
E_CODOMAIN
E_PRESERVATION
E_CONFLATION
E_STATUS
E_PROVENANCE
E_SCOPE
E_INVENTED_STRUCTURE
E_UNDECLARED_BASE_CHANGE
E_UNRESOLVED
```

```text
PASS
REJECT
REVIEW_REQUIRED
```

Examples:

```text
Bandwidth constrains Occupancy -> Bandwidth = Occupancy
REJECT / E_CONFLATION

coarse readout -> unique fine parent without provenance
REJECT / E_INVENTED_STRUCTURE

Chirality Fabric candidate inserted as canon

Connection -> characteristic class without bundle/coefficient structure
REVIEW_REQUIRED / E_UNRESOLVED

dependency arrow -> connection lift -> path transport -> holonomy audit
PASS
```

```text

OPERATOR SIGNATURE v0.1
DERIVATION ERROR TAXONOMY v0.1
QMO REVIEW GRAPH v0.1
```

```text
PASS -> active candidate graph
REVIEW_REQUIRED -> review graph
REJECT -> failed-derivation metadata
```

Nothing is discarded.

```text
TYPE_CORRECT != MATHEMATICALLY_TRUE
```
